<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi said:Ibn ‘Umar made the call to prayer on a cold and windy night. He then said: “Lo! Pray in the dwellings. “Afterwards he said: Whenever there was a cold or rainy day night, the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) used to command the mu’adhdin to announce: “Lo! Pray in the dwellings.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1063]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1063" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">नाफ़ेअ कहते हैं के इब्न-ए-उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने सर्द और आंधी वाली एक रात में नमाज़ के लिए अज़ान दी तो कहा: लोगो! डेरों में नमाज़ पढ़ लो, फिर उन्होंने कहा: रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) सर्दी या बारिश वाली रात होती तो मुअज़्ज़िन को यह एलान करने का हुक्म देते: लोगो! डेरों में नमाज़ पढ़ लो।</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi kehte hain ke Ibn-e-Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne sard aur aandhi wali ek raat mein namaz ke liye azan di to kaha: logo! deron mein namaz parh lo, phir unhon ne kaha: Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) sardi ya barish wali raat hoti to muazzin ko yeh elaan karne ka hukm dete: logo! deron mein namaz parh lo.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1063]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1063" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 621

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1064)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ النُّفَيْلِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ نَادَى مُنَادِي رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِذَلِكَ فِي الْمَدِينَةِ فِي اللَّيْلَةِ الْمَطِيرَةِ وَالْغَدَاةِ الْقَرَّةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ وَرَوَى هَذَا الْخَبَرَ يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ الأَنْصَارِيُّ عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ فِيهِ فِي السَّفَرِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1064 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Umar said:The announcer of the Messenger of Allah(ﷺ) announced for that (to pray at homes) at Medina on a rainy night or a cold morning. Abu Dawud said: This tradition has also been narrated by Yahya b. Sa’id al-Ansari from al-Qasim from Ibn ‘Umar from the Prophet (ﷺ). This version adds the words “During the journey.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के मुनादी ने मदीना के अंदर बारिश की रात या सर्दी की सुबह में ऐसी ही निदा की। अबू दाऊद कहते हैं: इस हदीस को यह्या बिन सईद अंसारी ने क़ासिम से, क़ासिम ने इब्न-ए-उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से, इब्न-ए-उमर ने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत किया है और इस में «फ़िस सफ़र» के अल्फ़ाज़ भी हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے منادی نے مدینہ کے اندر بارش کی رات یا سردی کی صبح میں ایسی ہی ندا کی۔ ابوداؤد کہتے ہیں: اس حدیث کو یحییٰ بن سعید انصاری نے قاسم سے، قاسم نے ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے، ابن عمر نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے روایت کیا ہے اور اس میں «في السفر» کے الفاظ بھی ہیں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke munadi ne Madina ke andar barish ki raat ya sardi ki subah mein aisi hi nida ki. Abu Dawood kehte hain: is hadith ko Yahya bin Saeed Ansari ne Qasim se, Qasim ne Ibn-e-Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se, Ibn-e-Umar ne Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se riwayat kiya hai aur is mein «fis safar» ke alfaaz bhi hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1064]</p>

## Narration 622

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1065)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا الْفَضْلُ بْنُ دُكَيْنٍ، حَدَّثَنَا زُهَيْرٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، قَالَ كُنَّا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي سَفَرٍ فَمُطِرْنَا فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لِيُصَلِّ مَنْ شَاءَ مِنْكُمْ فِي رَحْلِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1065" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1065 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir said:We were in the company of the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) during a journey. The rain fell upon us. The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) said: Anyone who wants to pray in his dwelling may pray</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1065" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1065 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं हम रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ एक सफ़र में थय के बारिश होने लगी तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम में से जो चाहे वह अपने डेरे में नमाज़ पढ़ ले.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1065" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جابر رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں ہم رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ ایک سفر میں تھے کہ بارش ہونے لگی تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: تم میں سے جو چاہے وہ اپنے ڈیرے میں نماز پڑھ لے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1065]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain hum Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath ek safar mein thay ke barish hone lagi to Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: tum mein se jo chahe woh apne dere mein namaz parh le.</p>

## Narration 623

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1067)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبَّاسُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَظِيمِ، حَدَّثَنِي إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ مَنْصُورٍ، حَدَّثَنَا هُرَيْمٌ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ الْمُنْتَشِرِ، عَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ، عَنْ طَارِقِ بْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الْجُمُعَةُ حَقٌّ وَاجِبٌ عَلَى كُلِّ مُسْلِمٍ فِي جَمَاعَةٍ إِلاَّ أَرْبَعَةً عَبْدٌ مَمْلُوكٌ أَوِ امْرَأَةٌ أَوْ صَبِيٌّ أَوْ مَرِيضٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ طَارِقُ بْنُ شِهَابٍ قَدْ رَأَى النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَلَمْ يَسْمَعْ مِنْهُ شَيْئًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Tariq ibn Shihab: The Prophet (ﷺ) said: The Friday prayer in congregation is a necessary duty for every Muslim, with four exceptions; a slave, a woman, a boy, and a sick person. Abu Dawud said: Tariq b. Shihab had seen the Prophet (ﷺ) but not heard anything from him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">तारिक़ बिन शिहाब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जुम्मे की नमाज़ जमात से ऐडा करना हर मुसलमान पर फ़र्ज़ है सिवाए चार लोगों के: घुलम कहते हैं: तारिक़ बिन शिहाब ने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को देखा है मगर उन्हों ने आप से कुछ सुना नहीं है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">طارق بن شہاب رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جمعہ کی نماز جماعت سے ادا کرنا ہر مسلمان پر فرض ہے سوائے چار لوگوں: غلام، عورت، نابالغ بچہ، اور بیمار کے ۔ ابوداؤد کہتے ہیں: طارق بن شہاب نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو دیکھا ہے مگر انہوں نے آپ سے کچھ سنا نہیں ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1067]</p>

Abu Dawood kehte hain: Tariq bin Shihab ne Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko dekha hai magar unhon ne aap se kuch suna nahi hai.</p>

## Narration 624

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1068)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، وَمُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الْمُخَرِّمِيُّ، - لَفْظُهُ - قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا وَكِيعٌ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ طَهْمَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي جَمْرَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ إِنَّ أَوَّلَ جُمُعَةٍ جُمِّعَتْ فِي الإِسْلاَمِ بَعْدَ جُمُعَةٍ جُمِّعَتْ فِي مَسْجِدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْمَدِينَةِ لَجُمُعَةٌ جُمِّعَتْ بِجُوَاثَاءَ قَرْيَةٍ مِنْ قُرَى الْبَحْرَيْنِ ‏.‏ قَالَ عُثْمَانُ قَرْيَةٌ مِنْ قُرَى عَبْدِ الْقَيْسِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1068" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1068 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Abbas said:The Friday prayer first offered in Islam after the Friday prayer offered in the mosque of the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) is Friday prayer offered at Juwatha, a village from the villages of al-Bahrain. The narrator ‘Uthman said: it is a village from the village of the tribe of ‘Abd al-Qais</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1068" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1068 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं इस्लाम में मस्जिद-ए-नबवी में जुम्मा क़ायम किये जाने के बाद पहला जुम्मा क़र्यह जुवाथा में क़ायम किया गया, जो इलाक़ा बहरीन का एक गाँव है। उस्मान कहते हैं: वो क़बीला अब्दुल क़ैस का एक गाँव है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1068" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما سے کہتے ہیں اسلام میں مسجد نبوی میں جمعہ قائم کئے جانے کے بعد پہلا جمعہ قریہ جواثاء میں قائم کیا گیا، جو علاقہ بحرین ۱؎ کا ایک گاؤں ہے، عثمان کہتے ہیں: وہ قبیلہ عبدالقیس کا ایک گاؤں ہے ۲؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1068]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain Islam mein Masjid-e-Nabvi mein Jumma qaim kiye jaane ke baad pehla Jumma qaryah Juwatha mein qaim kiya gaya, jo ilaaqa Bahrain ka ek gaon hai. Usman kehte hain: woh qabeela Abdul Qais ka ek gaon hai.</p>

## Narration 625

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1073)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُصَفَّى، وَعُمَرُ بْنُ حَفْصٍ الْوَصَّابِيُّ، - الْمَعْنَى - قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا بَقِيَّةُ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنِ الْمُغِيرَةِ الضَّبِّيِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ رُفَيْعٍ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدِ اجْتَمَعَ فِي يَوْمِكُمْ هَذَا عِيدَانِ فَمَنْ شَاءَ أَجْزَأَهُ مِنَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَإِنَّا مُجَمِّعُونَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ عُمَرُ عَنْ شُعْبَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated AbuHurayrah: The Prophet (ﷺ) said: Two festivals (&#x27;Id and Friday) have synchronised on this day. If anyone does not want to offer the Friday prayer, the &#x27;Id prayer is sufficient for him. But we shall offer the Friday prayer. This tradition has been narrated by &#x27;Umar from Shu&#x27;bah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम्हारे आज के इस दिन में दो ईदें इकट्ठा हो गयी हैं, लिहाज़ा रुख़्सत है, जो चाहे ईद उसे जुमा से काफ़ी होगी और हम तो जुमा पढ़ेंगे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: تمہارے آج کے اس دن میں دو عیدیں اکٹھا ہو گئی ہیں، لہٰذا رخصت ہے، جو چاہے عید اسے جمعہ سے کافی ہو گی اور ہم تو جمعہ پڑھیں گے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Tumhare aaj ke is din mein do Eidain ikattha ho gayi hain, lihaza rukhsat hai, jo chahe Eid usay Juma se kaafi hogi aur hum toh Juma parhenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
